Southwest Cannabis & Hemp Business Directory — AUDITED (Categories 1–12)

Region: Texas, New Mexico, Arizona, Nevada  |  Last audited: 31 October 2026 (audit checks run 31 October – 1 November 2026)  |  Original file: Southwest Cannabis & Hemp Business Directory — Complete (Categories 1–12), dated 31 October 2026, 177 unique businesses  |  This file: independent audit + corrected rewrite, single self-contained HTML document.

Scope and exclusions — read first. Local and regional businesses based in, or with a verified office in, TX, NM, AZ or NV that serve cannabis or hemp clients. General businesses count only where there is evidence they serve cannabis/hemp clients. Excluded in the original and in this rewrite: state-licensed cannabis operators — Texas Compassionate Use Program dispensing organisations; New Mexico Cannabis Control Division licensees; Arizona Department of Health Services licensees (marijuana establishments, nonprofit medical dispensaries, testing facilities); Nevada Cannabis Compliance Board licensees; and tribal cannabis enterprises in any of these states, including multi-state operators headquartered in the region. Hemp growers/processors and hemp product licence holders are Category 12 territory in the original; in this audit, growers/processors and plain resellers that do not make/brand finished products are moved to the separate Hemp licence only list below. National firms are included only with a verified local office in the region and are marked National. No Mexican business is listed; cross-border service is noted only as a fact about a US business. Verify / Unverified means a fact could not be confirmed on the business's own website in this audit, or sources conflict — do not treat it as confirmed. Only contact details published by the business for business purposes are shown; where none was published it says so. No private phone, email or home address was searched for or inferred. No licence, bar, CPA or insurance number in this file is regulator-verified unless the row says so; numbers labelled self-published, flyer-stated, OM-stated or job-posting-only are the firm's own claims, not registry results, and no number has been invented.
How to use the verdicts. Confirmed = own-site (or, for Categories 1–2, search-indexed official-site text) evidence supports the business, its cannabis/hemp service and the details carried over. Corrected = the business is kept, but an address, phone, email, tag, title, status or scope in the original was wrong or conflicted and is corrected/flagged in the rewritten entry. Unverified = kept, clearly flagged, because the cannabis/hemp connection, current operating status, or a contact detail could not be confirmed on the business's own site — do not present it as confirmed. Removed = taken out of the rewritten report (closed, acquired, or category inclusion with no cannabis evidence — the row says which). Hemp licence only = moved to the separate list below, not counted as a finished-product brand. Already listed = none assigned in this audit (see Overlap note below).

Audit verdict counts — reconciled to the original 177

Confirmed: 63  |  Corrected: 59  |  Unverified: 43  |  Removed: 8  |  Hemp licence only: 4  |  Already listed: 0  |  Total audited: 177. Kept in the rewritten report: 165 (Confirmed + Corrected + Unverified). Removed (8) + Hemp licence only (4) = 12 entries taken out of the main rewrite; 165 + 8 + 4 = 177. The original's 178th table row was an ARCO cross-reference row in Texas Category 5, not a second business, and is not counted here (ARCO is counted once, under Arizona Category 5).

Note on checker tallies: the four category checkers each reported their own subtotals; two subtotals contained arithmetic slips in their covering text (Categories 1–3 and 7–9). The counts above are recomputed from the 177 audit rows in this file itself, so the audit table, the counts and the rewrite agree. Where a checker called an entry 'Corrected' but its substance belongs elsewhere, this file says so explicitly: Soilutions is reclassified from Corrected to Removed (it is not a cannabis-waste vendor), and the four industrial hemp growers/processors/suppliers are classified Hemp licence only rather than left as Corrected brands.

Method and limits — please read before relying on any row

  • Own site first. Each business's own website was attempted first. Where a direct open was blocked, throttled (HTTP 429), returned only generic content, or failed, search-returned own-site crawl text was used as a secondary check and is labelled as such in the cover notes below. A blocked or throttled open is not treated as closure, and a search snippet alone was not upgraded into a live-visit claim.
  • Method differs by category — honestly. Categories 3, 4–6 (most entries) and 7–9 were checked with live browser opens. Categories 1–2 rest on search-indexed official-site page text because two delegated checkers failed on an inference-proxy 429 rate limit and the entries were re-checked from search-indexed official-site text; treat 'Confirmed' there as 'official-site text found', and re-open the Unverified and address-conflict entries live before publishing. Categories 10–12 rest on search-returned crawls, not separate live browser opens — the live-browser pass recommended below should directly open the own sites flagged there, especially Get Hoptimized, Anything Grows, Bodee Organics, Evergreen Tree, Power Biopharms and Eagle Moon.
  • Registries not systematically swept. No Secretary of State registry search (Texas SOSDirect / Comptroller franchise tax, New Mexico SOS, Arizona Corporation Commission, Nevada SilverFlume) returned a systematic record for most entries in this audit — those portals are interactive and returned no direct record in the checks run — so entity status is not confirmed from a registry for most rows, and no entry was removed solely for lack of a registry hit. The same applies to bar, CPA, contractor, security, insurance-producer and hemp licence lookups: where a lookup returned no result, the row says Unverified and names the lookup to try; no number was invented to fill the gap. The one hemp-list result actually returned in this audit is The Heart of Hemp on the NMED Approved Hemp Facilities list (updated 26 Oct 2026) — permit type Manufacturing, Albuquerque, no permit number published in the list excerpt.
  • Texas hemp caution. Texas hemp brands were checked for closure after the 31 March 2026 DSHS rules and the 31 July 2026 Delta-8 Schedule I date. No Texas brand in Category 12 was found closed in this audit — Hometown Hero and Wyatt Purp show active 2026 trading, OilWell's post-July range is unverified (not assumed closed), and CBIH's licence claim is press-release-level only. SOGO and GrayStone's post-2025/2026 Texas hemp appetite still needs direct broker confirmation.
  • Rate limits. Several opens returned HTTP 429 and were not retried (notably the Franklin Street cannabis service page, the Medina/Provenza listing page, the RESOLUT Bernalillo PDF, and Safety-Kleen). Those facts rest on search results or carry forward un-rechecked, as the row says. Re-open those four live before republication.
  • Same-name and place traps. Checked where the original flagged them: Las Vegas NV (not Las Vegas NM); Santa Fe NM (not Santa Fe TX); Glendale AZ / Peoria AZ vs CA/IL; Henderson NV vs KY/TN; Arlington/Pasadena/Paris/Athens TX vs other states; El Paso–Las Cruces, Texas Panhandle–eastern NM, Bullhead City–Laughlin, Lake Havasu, Yuma, Four Corners, Texarkana and Shreveport–East Texas (no Texarkana/Shreveport firm is counted in this directory — see Overlap note). Same-name firm traps are flagged in-row: Guillory Group (Michica) vs Guillory Real Estate Group (Keith); Blue Star Security Consultants (NM) vs Blue Star Security LLC (Chicago); 1st Security Services of Nevada vs 1st Integrated Solutions; Las Vegas Security Guards vs Las Vegas Security (lvsecurity.us); Interstate Electric (Los Lunas) vs Interstate Electric NM (Albuquerque); Pearson Engineering Associates (Phoenix) vs Pearson Engineering Ltd (UK); Evergreen Tree vs Evergreen Organix (licensed NV edibles producer, different firm); Libertas Insurance vs Liberty Company; Arizona Cannabis CPA (website brand) vs GreenGrowth CPAs' 'Arizona Cannabis CPA' marketing; Nevada Cannabis CPA vs cannabiscpa.tax guides; Premier Southwest Insurance vs Premier Southwest Planning Group; Roca Verde vs Red Rock Design Build (single-domain brand question, unresolved).

  • CropLine (Cat 8, NV) — LICENCE-BOUNDARY CANDIDATE. Its own site (cropline.io) states transportation runs 'under the Green Therapeutics NV CCB license' and its footer states 'DISTRIBUTION UNDER GREEN THERAPEUTICS · NV CCB LICENSE #62002429809099661968'. The number above is the firm's own published claim, not a CCB verification.
  • Miles Construction, president Cary Richardson (Cat 5, NV) — historical flag. NNBW (2018/2019) reported Richardson held a Nevada marijuana cultivation/production licence with MedMen. Current status unknown; no licence number found. Check the NV CCB list before any move-out decision.

Heart of Hemp's NMED hemp manufacturing permit is a hemp permit, not a cannabis licence, so it does not trigger a move-out. Staffing firms placing workers at licensees, banks banking CRBs, brokers/contractors/guards working at licensee sites, and guards holding a Cannabis Agent Card / CCB Card are vendor staffing/service, not firm licence-holding, on the evidence here. Overlap note — 'Already listed' (none assigned) and why

No entry in this audit is marked Already listed. The reason is stated plainly so the parent can overrule it if a stricter rule is wanted: no South Central or national directory was attached to this audit task — the only file attached was the Southwest complete directory itself. A name-match search was run against workspace copies of the audited South Central and national directories for transparency, and it found:

  • Name matches in the national directory (different scope/office, not treated as duplicates): Brink's, Inc. / PAI, Vangst, HUB International and Vicente LLP all appear in the national directory — but as national practices, not as the regional-office entries listed here (Brink's/PAI Coppell TX service, Vangst AZ jobs, HUB Albuquerque/Houston offices, Vicente Austin office). Treating a national-practice listing as the same entry as a regional-office listing would delete the regional detail this directory exists to give.
  • Herring Bank — do not conflate: the national directory's Herring Bank entry is described with a Raleigh, NC HQ, which does not match the Texas hemp-banking Herring Bank audited here (herringbank.com hemp-banking page, TX growers/processors/retailers/labs). That match is flagged, not merged, pending confirmation that they are the same bank.
  • South Central directory: HUB International appears there as a Little Rock, AR office (different office from the TX/NM offices here). Pittman Legal, Sergi & Associates, FACTSTX and Redbud Advisors were not found in the South Central audited file. No Texarkana or Shreveport firm is counted in the Southwest directory at all — the original's own notes confirm 'No Texarkana/Shreveport firm counted' and that 'TexaKana Organics' (Texas Green Craft, LLC, a 2026 DSHS-litigation plaintiff) is a brand name, not evidence of a Texarkana location, and was NOT counted.
  • Las Vegas exhibiting/headquartered firms: no systematic check against a Las Vegas exhibitor list was possible from the files attached; firms headquartered or exhibiting in Las Vegas that also appear in the national directory are covered by the name-match note above (Brink's/PAI, Vangst). If the parent wants a strict 'one entry across all directories' rule, the five name matches above are the candidates to re-examine first — but on the evidence here they are different offices/scopes, and this audit does not delete them on a name match alone.

Research notes carried over (from the original complete file)

The original file's Research Notes section (cross-border metro rules, same-name traps, deduplication notes, and starting sources not fully covered in Run 2) is summarised here because the tables above now carry its substance in-row: cross-border metros (El Paso–Las Cruces, Texas Panhandle–eastern NM, Bullhead City–Laughlin, Lake Havasu, Yuma, Four Corners, Texarkana, Shreveport–East Texas) — a business is listed under a state only if it has an office there; other states served are noted. Same-name traps checked in the original: Glendale AZ vs Glendale CA, Peoria AZ vs Peoria IL (no Peoria, AZ entry made in Run 2), Las Vegas NM vs Las Vegas NV, Santa Fe NM vs Santa Fe TX, Roswell NM vs Roswell GA, Clovis NM vs Clovis CA, Henderson NV vs Henderson KY/TN, Arlington TX vs Arlington VA, Pasadena TX vs Pasadena CA, Paris and Athens TX vs other states. No Mexican business is listed. Starting sources the original said were not fully covered in Run 2 (carry to any follow-up): AZ Dispensaries Association member/vendor list; NCIA member directory filtered to the region for Categories 7–10; US Hemp Roundtable / state hemp groups for Category 12. Deduplication carried over: BeachFleischman, Dark Horse CPAs, HUB International, MFE Insurance, ARCO, Brink's/PAI, GardaWorld, Loomis, US ATM Experts network, Stericycle, Cut Above Agency (NV + AZ) are each counted once, with every state served noted in-row; RESOLUT RE and Blue Star Security are each listed once in their home state with cross-border service noted, not in both states.
